Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on analytical structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 538. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 433 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the analytical decomposition. By minimality, the element a^538 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 433-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
